HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001","root_000476/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0b4caf1ebfbd308c593a","text":"1. **Failure as burial of the self's visible truth.** Lexical evidence: خ ي ب B001 \"missing the sought thing and being deprived of fortune\"; د س و B001 \"hidden insertion or concealment\"; ض ح و B002 open exposure in 91:1; ج ل و B001 uncovering in 91:3. Structural cue: the loser is paired against the purifier and after a chain of unveiling images. Assigned role: دسّى is the counter-act to cosmic disclosure: hiding what should stand in light. Abductive move: I infer that failure results when the soul's inspired distinction is pushed under cover; the packet supplies failure, concealment, and exposure branches. Reading change: the ayah becomes an anti-epiphany: loss is self-concealment against the world of unveiling.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_14b145ce2c902124b318","text":"2. **Failure as fire-stick without spark.** Lexical evidence: خ ي ب B002 \"a fire-stick that gives no spark\"; ش م س B001 sunlight; ف ج ر B002 dawn; ل ه م B005 engulfing calamity. Structural cue: the surah begins with intense light but the failing person cannot kindle benefit from it. Assigned role: khayb B002 provides a concrete mechanism: a tool expected to produce fire fails to ignite. Abductive move: I infer that the self has access to light but cannot generate living response because it buried the capacity; the packet supplies light-sequence and no-spark failure. Reading change: خاب is not generic disappointment but wasted ignition in a light-filled cosmos.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000476/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_340454b127be8454cc9c","text":"3. **Dass as corrupt insertion rather than mere hiding.** Lexical evidence: د س و B003 \"straying and spoiling\"; ل ه م B001 inspired intake in 91:8; ك ذ ب B009 deceptive variegated cloth in 91:11/14. Structural cue: ألهمها puts moral knowledge into the soul; دسّىها later inserts/hides something corrupting. Assigned role: دسو B003 makes the act an adulteration of the soul's received contents. Abductive move: I infer a rival implantation: corrupt concealment competes with inspired intake; the packet supplies both verbs' inward mechanics. Reading change: the ayah reads as sabotage of inner composition, not only neglect.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000476/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3e1bf499f41ff388deb4","text":"1. **Failure by hiding an already informed self.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د س و/B001` is hidden insertion or concealment and fixed `خ ي ب/B001` is missing what was sought and gaining no good. **Structural cue and role:** the object is the self already formed and informed in 91:7-8, and concealment directly reverses 91:9's cultivation; hiding is therefore the mechanism that produces deprivation. **Abductive move:** sequence and opposition are explicit, while the causal arrow \"concealment blocks the sought good\" is inferred. **Reading change:** failure is not ignorance but the active burial of known capacity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5961b96b1e7cdf830522","text":"91:11-14 exposed concealment as a social sequence: denial, mobilization, warning rejected, and bodily harm. 91:12 initially resembles ignition, but its destructive direction corrects the spark image: motion alone is not successful activation. 91:15 finally verifies `خاب` by naming the aftermath in which the failed trajectory is measured.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000476/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_637810b4a9f2c57037a9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:10:4\",\"root_000476\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Canın içten bozulması, sonraki inkâr, harekete geçme ve şiddet dizisinde özel bir kusurun toplumsal zarara dönüşen gizli aşaması olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_76083ce0478701ee2a48","text":"4. **Failure disclosed by its eventual aftermath.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `خ ي ب/B001` is deprivation of the sought result; 91:15 `ع ق ب/B006` is the end or aftermath an act brings after it. **Structural cue and role:** `خاب` precedes a narrative whose consequence closes on `عقباها`, so the end functions as the delayed proof of failure. **Abductive move:** the packet gives initial verdict and final outcome; taking the latter as evidentiary disclosure of the former is inferred. **Reading change:** failure is measured by what the act ultimately leaves behind, not by its immediate appearance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9b49a33e8f14f6adfab1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:10:2\",\"root_000451\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kıvılcım vermeyen çubuk, ışık ve iç bilgiyle donatılmış bir imkânın görünür bir eyleme hiç dönüşmemesini somutlaştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a609d9066ba7e9c22a9b","text":"2. **A fire-making implement that never catches.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `خ ي ب/B002` is a fire-stick that gives no spark; 91:8 `ف ج ر/B002` carries dawn breaking from darkness, and the self has already taken its distinctions inward through `ألهمها`. **Structural cue and role:** latent light is followed by a failed outcome, so the sparkless implement models discernment that never becomes visible action. **Abductive move:** no fire is stated; mapping inspiration to tinder and action to ignition is a material analogy inferred from the branches. **Reading change:** `خاب` becomes frustrated activation: endowed potential fails to kindle.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_abb6dc448f2c28394a57","text":"91:11–14 clarified that دسّىها is not private self-failure only. Thamud's public denial and bodily violence show concealment turning outward as falsification of a sign. The surprise was خ ي ب B002: after all the light imagery, failure as a fire-stick that gives no spark became a compact image for moral non-response.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001","root_000476/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_de383d0e37850c3fe68b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:10:2\",\"root_000451\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:10:4\",\"root_000476\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kayıp, daha önce biçimlendirilip bilgilendirilmiş canın hakikatini etkin biçimde gömmek ve onun görünür olmasını engellemekle gerçekleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000476/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dfc3b2f02896edffecb0","text":"3. **Inner spoilage becoming collective motion.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `د س و/B003` is leading astray and spoiling; 91:11 supplies collective denial, 91:12 a representative who sets out, and 91:14 the completed violent act. **Structural cue and role:** an inwardly directed verb is immediately followed by social subject, mobilization, and action, so self-corruption serves as the latent stage of public harm. **Abductive move:** the later sequence is supplied; the direction from corrupted self to collective violence is inferred rather than stated. **Reading change:** `دساها` has a social causal horizon, not only a private inward one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f74d280055da6e94f167","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:10:2\",\"root_000451\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kayıp hükmünün doğruluğu hemen görünen başarıyla değil, eylemin sonunda geride bıraktığı yıkıcı sonuçla ölçülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
